- **integrity-audit**: 7-mode AI failure-mode checklist applied before publishing any ORS artifact (skill, manuscript section, dataset card, agent output). - **intensity-scaling-visualization**: Use when MS/MS spectra (e.g. from an MGF file such as iPRG2012.mgf) need intensity de-emphasis of overly intense peaks and downstream rendering as static publication figures or interactive web plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/intensity-scaling-visualization))
- **intensity-season-maintenance**: 当个人在某个领域取得显著进展或进入高效产出期（“强度季节”），希望系统化成果以避免倒退时 ([DracoUnion/dankoe-skills](https://github.com/DracoUnion/dankoe-skills/tree/HEAD/intensity-season-maintenance) · ★14)
- **intensity-threshold-background-segmentation**: Use when a spatial metabolomics peak table (X/Y pixel coordinates plus m/z intensity columns) contains off-tissue background pixels that degrade clustering, and a total-intensity threshold must be derived or applied to segment tissue from background. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/intensity-threshold-background-segmentation))
- **intensity-threshold-baseline-comparison**: Use when a binary signal/noise classifier (e.g. LSH on mass-spectrometry windows) needs a ROC-based comparison against intensity/SNR threshold filtering on labeled synthetic windows with per-panel intensity settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/intensity-threshold-baseline-comparison))
- **intensity-threshold-evaluation**: Use when you have processed LC-MS data (mzML or vendor format) and need to validate that internal standards and target analytes produce peak intensities within expected operational ranges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/intensity-threshold-evaluation) · 2 copies)
- **intensity-threshold-filtering-ms-data**: Use when when you have loaded aligned peak data (from a preceding molecular networking alignment task) as a structured table with peak intensity, m/z, retention time, and alignment quality metrics, and you need to reduce false positives, remove noise, or focus analysis on peaks above a. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/intensity-threshold-filtering-ms-data) · 2 copies)
- **intensity-threshold-navigation**: 当个人在追求明确目标的过程中，经过一段高强度、高清晰度的“心流”工作期后，发现努力带来的边际收益显著下降，即将或已经陷入停滞时，调用此模型 ([DracoUnion/dankoe-skills](https://github.com/DracoUnion/dankoe-skills/tree/HEAD/intensity-threshold-navigation) · ★14)
- **intensity-threshold-noise-filtering**: Use when you have loaded a raw or partially processed MsmsSpectrum object and need to reduce spectral noise before annotation, matching, or visualization. Use it especially when spectra contain many weak peaks (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/intensity-threshold-noise-filtering) · 3 copies)
- **intensity-threshold-peak-localization**: Use when you need to locate peak clusters in simulated (or preprocessed) 1D 1H, 2D J-Res, or 2D COSY 1H NMR spectra before further modeling, using only a user-supplied intensity cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/intensity-threshold-peak-localization))
- **intensity-to-absolute-concentration-conversion**: Use when your lipidomics experiment includes spiked internal lipid standards of known concentration, and you have raw signal intensity matrices from LipidSearch or LIQUID output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/intensity-to-absolute-concentration-conversion) · 2 copies)
- **intensity-to-concentration-conversion**: Use when you have raw mass spectrometry intensity measurements from sample analyses and need absolute quantitative concentrations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/intensity-to-concentration-conversion) · 2 copies)
- **intensity-to-concentration-conversion**: Use when you have raw MS intensity data paired with measurements from known concentration standards, and you need to produce absolute quantified concentration values rather than relative intensity measurements. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/intensity-to-concentration-conversion))
- **intensity-value-aggregation-across-replicates**: Use when after peak recognition has identified features (m/z and retention time pairs) across one or more MS replicates, and you need to collapse multiple intensity measurements per feature into a single representative value per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/intensity-value-aggregation-across-replicates) · 2 copies)
- **intensity-vector-manipulation-numpy**: Use when you have extracted mass tracks (EICs) from multiple LC-MS samples aligned into a MassGrid structure, and you need to combine their intensity vectors into a single composite intensity vector for peak detection on the aggregate signal rather than per-sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/intensity-vector-manipulation-numpy) · 2 copies)
- **intensity-weighted-gaussian-mz-density-estimation**: Use when reconstructing or tuning the mass trace detection stage of LC-MS feature finding from centroided mzML data, specifically when deciding which nearby peaks belong to a seeded trace along the m/z axis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/intensity-weighted-gaussian-mz-density-estimation))
- **intensity-weighted-positional-encoding**: Use when reimplementing or extending the IDSL_MINT MS2Fingerprint transformer — specifically when encoding .msp MS/MS peak lists (m/z + intensity pairs) as transformer input sequences with intensity-aware positional embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/intensity-weighted-positional-encoding))
- **intensity-weighted-spectral-peak-matching**: Use when annotating a tandem MS spectrum by candidate retrieval: you have an experimental MS/MS peak list (m/z, intensity) and candidate molecules whose theoretical fragmentation patterns must be matched and scored. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/intensity-weighted-spectral-peak-matching))
- **intensity-weighted-spectrum-vector-scoring**: Use when comparing MS/MS query spectra against a library (e.g. cleaned AllPositive GNPS dataset) and peak-based cosine scores underperform, or when you need similarity scores that track structural similarity across instruments and conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/intensity-weighted-spectrum-vector-scoring))
